## Document 008: 10月非农：就业市场短期修复，但结构性分化与长期失业压力仍存

10 月非农：就业市场短期修复，但结构性分化与
长期失业压力仍存

宏观经济点评
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 2
近期有所走强，主要集中在会计师事务所和咨询机构。根据褐皮书的调
查，高利率环境叠加关税成本上升，推动破产相关法律服务需求增加。不
过，机构对后续业务增长仍持相对谨慎的态度，预计整体业务仅温和增
长。经济和政治不确定性，以及燃油、商品成本上升，或促使部分客户收
缩支出。此外，本月较高的就业增量可能受到选举相关活动的短期推动。
波士顿学院经济学家布莱恩·贝休恩认为，上月专业和商业服务就业激增
可能存在一定的选举因素：各竞选团队增加了对咨询、广告及临时用工的
投入，随着相关活动结束，部分岗位需求可能随之消退，预计 11 月相关
机构或裁员约 2 万人。因此，若这一判断成立，10 月专业和商业服务的
高增长可能在后续月份出现部分回吐，其持续性仍有待观察。
二是医疗和社会救助行业的就业有所回升，政策变化可能带来中期上行空
间。医疗和社会救助新增 5.5 万人，较此前有所改善，呈现一定的触底回
升迹象。其中，医疗保健服务增加 4.2 万人，高于过去 12 个月 3.1 万人

的月均水平；社会援助增加 1.4 万人。相较于 10 月的就业增量，后续政
策变化可能是影响该行业中期就业走势的另一项重要变量。中期选举后，
民主党取得参众两院控制权，医疗可负担性议题可能获得更高的立法优先
级。选前调查显示，医疗负担能力是选民关注的重要议题之一，相关选民
诉求可能推动民主党加强对《平价医疗法案》（ACA）和医疗补助计划
（Medicaid）相关政策的监督，并尝试通过立法缓解保费和药价负担。若
相关政策调整得以推进，医疗服务需求及相关就业可能获得额外支撑，医
疗行业就业的中期增长中枢或有所上移。
三是假日季前补员或带动下游就业，但上游批发却有所走弱。运输和仓
储、零售与批发均属于贸易、运输和公用事业大类。10 月这几个行业的
就业变化，可能在一定程度上反映了假日购物季前的季节性招聘，但上下
游表现并不一致。运输和仓储增加 2.5 万人，为 4 月以来最大单月增幅，
其中快递及邮政投递增加 1.7 万人。Challenger 数据显示，亚马逊 10 月
公布的 30.0 万个招聘计划几乎全部属于季节性岗位，主要集中在仓储和

电商物流领域。因此，本月运输仓储就业增长可能更多反映企业为假日购
物季提前补充人手，而非物流需求出现趋势性扩张。零售业增加 0.6 万
人，连续第八个月实现增长。分项来看，综合商品零售商和服装、服饰配
件、鞋类及珠宝零售商是主要支撑。其中，百货商店增加 0.7 万人，仓储
式会员店、大型综合超市及其他综合商品零售商合计增加 1.2 万人，服
装、服饰配件、鞋类及珠宝零售商增加 1.3 万人。不过，零售就业增长同
样带有较强的季节性特征。Challenger 指出，部分零售商在裁减长期岗位
的同时，为假日购物季增加临时招聘，反映企业可能更倾向于通过季节性
用工应对短期需求，而非扩大长期雇佣规模。与运输仓储和零售业的补员
形成对比的是，批发业在连续六个月增长后，于 10 月减少 0.9 万人。其
中，耐用品批发商减少 0.3 万人，非耐用品批发商减少 0.8 万人。
四是其他行业表现仍存结构性分化：信息、金融业边际改善，休闲酒店业
有所走弱。信息业减少 0.1 万人，但近三个月就业降幅有所收窄，行业内

此前负增长转为正增长。其中，金融及保险业增加 0.1 万人，房地产、租
赁及出租业增加 0.6 万人。本月信息业就业降幅收窄、金融业就业转正，
可能反映部分细分领域招聘需求有所改善。不过，信息业和金融业均受到
AI 技术发展的影响较多，目前单月数据尚不足以确认行业就业趋势已经反
转，后续仍需观察改善能否持续。休闲及酒店业减少 1.3 万人，主要受到
餐饮服务就业回落的影响。此前两个月，餐饮服务就业累计增加 4.3 万
人。但是餐饮服务业短期波动相对明显，因此本月就业下降可能包含前期
招聘增长后的季节性回吐，暂时还不足以据此判断居民服务消费需求明显
走弱。
商品业中主要是制造业支撑就业增长，新增岗位集中于 AI 相关耐用品。
商品部门新增就业约 1.5 万人，增量几乎全部来自制造业，增加 1.9 万
人，连续第五个月实现增长，而非耐用品制造业减少 0.6 万人。从需求端
看，制造业景气度也有所改善。10 月 ISM 制造业 PMI 升至 55.5，高于 9

月的 54.5 及市场预期的 54.6，创近四年来第二高的扩张水平。其中，生
产指数由 56.7 升至 59.0，新订单指数由 55.3 小幅升至 55.5。生产与新订
单指数同步处于扩张区间，表明制造业供需两端均有所改善，短期内或继
续为制造业就业提供支撑。从行业结构来看，计算机及电子产品、金属制
品和机械设备三个行业新增就业较多，合计增加 1.6 万人，或与人工智
能、数据中心、半导体及相关基础设施领域的需求增长有关。
政府就业里主要是州政府提供支撑，联邦与地方政府就业小幅收缩。政府
部门新增就业 0.6 万人，仅占全部新增非农就业的约 4%，对本月整体就
业增长的贡献相对有限。从内部结构来看，政府就业呈现分化：州政府增
加 0.8 万人，联邦政府和地方政府则分别减少 0.1 万人。
劳动力市场供需两端边际改善，但就业结构仍存分化，长期失业压力上
升。
美国劳动力需求出现边际改善，企业裁员意愿偏低，招聘活动有所回暖。
Challenger 数据显示，10 月美国雇主宣布裁员 43,994 人，略高于 9 月的

43,281 人，但较 2025 年 10 月的 153,074 人大幅下降 71%，为 2023 年
以来同月最低水平。从招聘需求来看，Indeed 招聘广告数量自 6 月触底
后缓慢回升，但扩张力度仍然有限。此外，企业也可能通过调整员工工时
而非直接裁员来应对需求变化。10 月私人非农平均每周工时维持 34.4 小
时，为 2024 年 3 月以来最高水平，企业可能通过提高现有员工的劳动投
入来满足生产需求；不过制造业平均每周工时为 40.5 小时，加班时间增
加 0.1 小时至 3.1 小时，显示需求与资本品订单的边际拉动有限。
潜在劳动力供给有所累积，就业意愿与求职信心出现背离。10 月劳动参
与率由 61.8%回升至 61.9%，劳动力人口增加 42.1 万人，表明劳动力供
给有所扩张。其中，25—54 岁核心劳动年龄人口劳动参与率为 83.7%。
与此同时，非劳动力人口内部仍存在一定的潜在供给。非劳动力人口中希
望就业但未积极求职人口的人数占比由 53.5%升至 54.5%，显示有意愿就

宏观经济点评
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 4
升至 0.43%，反映部分潜在求职者可能因就业机会不足或求职受挫而暂时
退出劳动力市场。
从就业结构来看，总量改善之下，质量仍存在分化。10 月就业人口增加
46.3 万人，高于劳动力人口增幅，意味着当月就业增长不仅吸收了新增劳
动力供给，也消化了部分存量失业人口，推动失业率由 4.2%降至 4.1%。
劳动参与率同步回升，说明失业率下降并非单纯由劳动者退出劳动力市场
所推动，就业市场总量表现有所改善。全职就业人数自 7 月近期低点以来
波动上行，显示全职岗位有所修复，但恢复过程仍不稳定。部分劳动者需
要通过同时从事多份工作来增加收入，多重职业者占总就业人口的比例升
至 5.4%，仍处于 2024 年以来 5.1%—5.5%的区间上沿。与此同时，因经
济原因从事兼职的人数环比增加 25.7 万人，反映部分劳动者仍面临全职
岗位不足或工作时长受限的问题。
从失业结构与失业时长来看，主动离职意愿偏弱，长期失业压力上升。主
动离职者占失业人口的比例由 10.5%升至 10.9%，但仍低于过去 12 个月

11.8%的均值，说明劳动者主动辞职、寻找更好工作机会的意愿尚未明显
恢复。重新进入劳动力市场者人数小幅升至 3.28 万人，高于过去 12 个月
均值，反映部分此前退出劳动力市场的人群正在重新求职。与此同时，长
期失业压力仍在累积，部分失业者重新就业的难度可能有所增加，劳动力
市场内部的就业再配置压力尚未明显缓解。10 月失业 27 周以上的人数增
加 1.9 万人至 196.3 万人，占失业人口的比例升至 27.6%，为 2021 年底
以来最高水平；失业中位时长升至 11.9 周，平均失业时长达到 27 周。
工资增长呈现环比反弹、同比放缓的分化特征。10 月私人非农平均时薪
环比上涨 0.37%，为 2025 年 11 月以来最大单月涨幅，后续仍需关注工
资环比增速能否持续维持高位；同比上涨 3.12%，仍处于缓慢回落趋势。
分行业来看，建筑业时薪环比上涨 0.91%，涨幅相对突出；制造业上涨
0.49%；专业和商业服务、教育和健康服务均上涨 0.33%。
就业短期修复提振市场，但结构性分化与持续性仍待观察。

10 月就业数据公布后，美股内部结构分化，利率重新定价与 AI 产业逻辑
交织。纳斯达克综合指数上涨 1.09%，道琼斯工业平均指数下跌 0.46%，
标普 500 指数上涨 0.31%，科技成长股明显跑赢，三大指数表现分化。债
市方面，2 年期和 10 年期美债收益率分别上行 4.9bp 和 2.4bp，收益率曲
线熊平，或反映市场对美联储降息路径重新定价，短端利率上行压力更
大。与此同时，美元指数上涨 0.68%，COMEX 黄金下跌 0.83%。整体来
看，10 月美国劳动力市场供需两端均有所改善，新增就业明显回升，失
业率下降，劳动参与率同步上升，显示就业市场短期修复力度有所增强。
但就业增长仍集中于少数行业，长期失业压力上升、非自愿兼职增加，表
明就业市场尚未实现全面、均衡的改善。往后看，就业市场回暖的持续性
仍需进一步观察，一方面，随着假日季临时招聘及选举相关用工需求逐步
消退，需关注就业增量能否保持韧性，以及招聘需求能否向更多行业扩
散；另一方面，需关注工资环比增速反弹能否延续，以及长期失业、非自

1. 就业市场短期修复，但结构性分化与长期失业压力仍存 
1.1 10 月美国就业市场明显走强，劳动力供需同步改善 
10 月美国非农就业新增 16.5 万人，大幅高于市场预期的 6.5 万人，也高于过去 12
个月 6.1 万人的月均增幅。与此同时，8 月和 9 月新增就业人数分别由 13.3 万人和 2.9
万人上修至 18.6 万人和 6.0 万人，合计上修 8.4 万人，推动近三个月新增就业均值升至
13.7 万人，为 2025 年 2 月以来最高，显示就业市场修复力度有所增强。家庭调查数据
同样表现强劲。10 月就业人口增加 46.3 万人，高于上月的 40.6 万人；U3 失业率由 4.2%
降至 4.1%，劳动参与率由 61.8%升至 61.9%。延续 7 月以来的回升趋势。劳动力参与
率回升的同时失业率有所下降，或反映劳动力市场需求有所改善。就业数据公布后，市
场对美联储政策路径的预期偏鹰：12 月加息一次的概率升至 78.8%；对于 2027 年 6 月
的政策路径，市场定价在此前加息基础上再加息一次的概率为 38.9%，维持利率不变的
概率为 33.9%，两者差距较小，表明市场虽倾向于进一步加息，但对后续政策路径仍存
在较大分歧。 
1.2 行业扩散有所改善，但新增就业仍集中于少数领域，结构性分化明显 
10 月非农新增就业 16.5 万人，其中私人部门增加 15.9 万人，政府部门增加 0.6 万
人，新增就业几乎全部来自私人部门。而私人部门新增就业主要由服务部门贡献，增加
14.4 万人。从就业扩散情况看，行业扩散指数由 9 月修正后的 54.2 升至 57.6，三个月
均值也升至今年以来最高水平，表明新增就业涉及的行业范围有所扩大。不过，从具体
行业贡献来看，就业增长仍存在明显的结构性分化：专业和商业服务、医疗和社会救助、
运输和仓储三大行业合计新增约 15 万人，占全部新增非农就业的九成以上，表明本轮
就业增长仍主要由少数行业支撑，尚未形成广泛、均衡的招聘回暖。 
服务业方面主要呈现四大特征： 
一是专业和商业服务贡献最大，但增量可能包含一次性因素。专业和商业服务新增
7.0 万人，占全部新增非农就业的 42.4%，为 2022 年 7 月以来最大单月增幅，明显高于
过去 12 个月 1.5 万人的月均水平。分项来看，专业、科学和技术服务增加 3.3 万人，其
中管理、科学和技术咨询服务贡献 1.7 万人；行政和支持服务增加 2.8 万人，其中临时
帮助服务增加 1.9 万人；企业管理增加 0.9 万人。从需求端反馈来看，专业和商业服务
需求近期有所走强，主要集中在会计师事务所和咨询机构。根据褐皮书的调查，高利率
环境叠加关税成本上升，推动破产相关法律服务需求增加。不过，机构对后续业务增长
仍持相对谨慎的态度，预计整体业务仅温和增长。经济和政治不确定性，以及燃油、商
品成本上升，或促使部分客户收缩支出1。此外，本月较高的就业增量可能受到选举相关
活动的短期推动。波士顿学院经济学家布莱恩·贝休恩认为，上月专业和商业服务就业激
增可能存在一定的选举因素：各竞选团队增加了对咨询、广告及临时用工的投入，随着
相关活动结束，部分岗位需求可能随之消退，预计 11 月相关机构或裁员约 2 万人2。因
此，若这一判断成立，10 月专业和商业服务的高增长可能在后续月份出现部分回吐，其
持续性仍有待观察。 
二是医疗和社会救助行业的就业有所回升，政策变化可能带来中期上行空间。医疗
和社会救助新增 5.5 万人，较此前有所改善，呈现一定的触底回升迹象。其中，医疗保
健服务增加 4.2 万人，高于过去 12 个月 3.1 万人的月均水平；社会援助增加 1.4 万人。
相较于 10 月的就业增量，后续政策变化可能是影响该行业中期就业走势的另一项重要
变量。中期选举后，民主党取得参众两院控制权，医疗可负担性议题可能获得更高的立
法优先级。选前调查显示，医疗负担能力是选民关注的重要议题之一，相关选民诉求可
能推动民主党加强对《平价医疗法案》（ACA）和医疗补助计划（Medicaid）相关政策的

### Page 9

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 9 
监督，并尝试通过立法缓解保费和药价负担3。若相关政策调整得以推进，医疗服务需求
及相关就业可能获得额外支撑，医疗行业就业的中期增长中枢或有所上移。 
三是假日季前补员或带动下游就业，但上游批发却有所走弱。运输和仓储、零售与
批发均属于贸易、运输和公用事业大类。10 月这几个行业的就业变化，可能在一定程度
上反映了假日购物季前的季节性招聘，但上下游表现并不一致。运输和仓储增加 2.5 万
人，为 4 月以来最大单月增幅，其中快递及邮政投递增加 1.7 万人。Challenger 数据显
示，亚马逊 10 月公布的 30.0 万个招聘计划几乎全部属于季节性岗位，主要集中在仓储
和电商物流领域4。因此，本月运输仓储就业增长可能更多反映企业为假日购物季提前补
充人手，而非物流需求出现趋势性扩张。零售业增加 0.6 万人，连续第八个月实现增长。
分项来看，综合商品零售商和服装、服饰配件、鞋类及珠宝零售商是主要支撑。其中，
百货商店增加 0.7 万人，仓储式会员店、大型综合超市及其他综合商品零售商合计增加
1.2 万人，服装、服饰配件、鞋类及珠宝零售商增加 1.3 万人。不过，零售就业增长同样
带有较强的季节性特征。Challenger 指出，部分零售商在裁减长期岗位的同时，为假日
购物季增加临时招聘，反映企业可能更倾向于通过季节性用工应对短期需求，而非扩大
长期雇佣规模。与运输仓储和零售业的补员形成对比的是，批发业在连续六个月增长后，
于 10 月减少 0.9 万人。其中，耐用品批发商减少 0.3 万人，非耐用品批发商减少 0.8 万
人。 
四是其他行业表现仍存结构性分化：信息、金融业边际改善，休闲酒店业有所走弱。
信息业减少 0.1 万人，但近三个月就业降幅有所收窄，行业内部也出现一定分化。其中，
计算基础设施、数据处理、网络托管及相关服务就业持平，电影及录音产业增加 0.3 万
人。金融业则增加 0.7 万人，由此前负增长转为正增长。其中，金融及保险业增加 0.1
万人，房地产、租赁及出租业增加 0.6 万人。本月信息业就业降幅收窄、金融业就业转
正，可能反映部分细分领域招聘需求有所改善。不过，信息业和金融业均受到 AI 技术发
展的影响较多，目前单月数据尚不足以确认行业就业趋势已经反转，后续仍需观察改善
能否持续。休闲及酒店业减少 1.3 万人，主要受到餐饮服务就业回落的影响。此前两个
月，餐饮服务就业累计增加 4.3 万人。但是餐饮服务业短期波动相对明显，因此本月就
业下降可能包含前期招聘增长后的季节性回吐，暂时还不足以据此判断居民服务消费需
求明显走弱。 
商品业中主要是制造业支撑就业增长，新增岗位集中于 AI 相关耐用品。商品部门
新增就业约 1.5 万人，增量几乎全部来自制造业，增加 1.9 万人，连续第五个月实现增
长，而非耐用品制造业减少 0.6 万人。从需求端看，制造业景气度也有所改善。10 月 ISM
制造业 PMI 升至 55.5，高于 9 月的 54.5 及市场预期的 54.6，创近四年来第二高的扩张
水平。其中，生产指数由 56.7 升至 59.0，新订单指数由 55.3 小幅升至 55.5。生产与新
订单指数同步处于扩张区间，表明制造业供需两端均有所改善，短期内或继续为制造业
就业提供支撑。从行业结构来看，计算机及电子产品、金属制品和机械设备三个行业新
增就业较多，合计增加 1.6 万人，或与人工智能、数据中心、半导体及相关基础设施领
域的需求增长有关。 
政府就业里主要是州政府提供支撑，联邦与地方政府就业小幅收缩。政府部门新增
就业 0.6 万人，仅占全部新增非农就业的约 4%，对本月整体就业增长的贡献相对有限。
从内部结构来看，政府就业呈现分化：州政府增加 0.8 万人，联邦政府和地方政府则分
别减少 0.1 万人。 
1.3 劳动力市场供需两端边际改善，但就业结构仍存分化，长期失业压力
上升 
美国劳动力需求出现边际改善，企业裁员意愿偏低，招聘活动有所回暖。Challenger

### Page 10

宏观经济点评
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 10
数据显示，10 月美国雇主宣布裁员 43,994 人，略高于 9 月的 43,281 人，但较 2025 年
10 月的 153,074 人大幅下降 71%，为 2023 年以来同月最低水平。从招聘需求来看，
Indeed 招聘广告数量自 6 月触底后缓慢回升，但扩张力度仍然有限。此外，企业也可能
通过调整员工工时而非直接裁员来应对需求变化。10 月私人非农平均每周工时维持 34.4
小时，为 2024 年 3 月以来最高水平，企业可能通过提高现有员工的劳动投入来满足生
产需求；不过制造业平均每周工时为 40.5 小时，加班时间增加 0.1 小时至 3.1 小时，显
示需求与资本品订单的边际拉动有限。
潜在劳动力供给有所累积，就业意愿与求职信心出现背离。10 月劳动参与率由 61.8%
回升至 61.9%，劳动力人口增加 42.1 万人，表明劳动力供给有所扩张。其中，25—54
岁核心劳动年龄人口劳动参与率为 83.7%。与此同时，非劳动力人口内部仍存在一定的

潜在供给。非劳动力人口中希望就业但未积极求职人口的人数占比由 53.5%升至 54.5%，
显示有意愿就业但尚未进入劳动力市场的人群有所增加，后续若就业机会改善，部分非
劳动力人口或重新进入劳动力市场。与此同时，失望个人占比从 0.37%上升至 0.43%，
反映部分潜在求职者可能因就业机会不足或求职受挫而暂时退出劳动力市场。
从就业结构来看，总量改善之下，质量仍存在分化。10 月就业人口增加 46.3 万人，
高于劳动力人口增幅，意味着当月就业增长不仅吸收了新增劳动力供给，也消化了部分
存量失业人口，推动失业率由 4.2%降至 4.1%。劳动参与率同步回升，说明失业率下降
并非单纯由劳动者退出劳动力市场所推动，就业市场总量表现有所改善。全职就业人数
自 7 月近期低点以来波动上行，显示全职岗位有所修复，但恢复过程仍不稳定。部分劳
动者需要通过同时从事多份工作来增加收入，多重职业者占总就业人口的比例升至 5.4%，
仍处于 2024 年以来 5.1%—5.5%的区间上沿。与此同时，因经济原因从事兼职的人数

环比增加 25.7 万人，反映部分劳动者仍面临全职岗位不足或工作时长受限的问题。
从失业结构与失业时长来看，主动离职意愿偏弱，长期失业压力上升。主动离职者
占失业人口的比例由 10.5%升至 10.9%，但仍低于过去 12 个月 11.8%的均值，说明劳
动者主动辞职、寻找更好工作机会的意愿尚未明显恢复。重新进入劳动力市场者人数小
幅升至 3.28 万人，高于过去 12 个月均值，反映部分此前退出劳动力市场的人群正在重
新求职。与此同时，长期失业压力仍在累积，部分失业者重新就业的难度可能有所增加，
劳动力市场内部的就业再配置压力尚未明显缓解。10 月失业 27 周以上的人数增加 1.9
万人至 196.3 万人，占失业人口的比例升至 27.6%，为 2021 年底以来最高水平；失业
中位时长升至 11.9 周，平均失业时长达到 27 周。
工资增长呈现环比反弹、同比放缓的分化特征。10 月私人非农平均时薪环比上涨
0.37%，为 2025 年 11 月以来最大单月涨幅，后续仍需关注工资环比增速能否持续维持

高位；同比上涨 3.12%，仍处于缓慢回落趋势。分行业来看，建筑业时薪环比上涨 0.91%，
涨幅相对突出；制造业上涨 0.49%；专业和商业服务、教育和健康服务均上涨 0.33%。
1.4 就业短期修复提振市场，但结构性分化与持续性仍待观察
10 月就业数据公布后，美股内部结构分化，利率重新定价与 AI 产业逻辑交织。纳
斯达克综合指数上涨 1.09%，道琼斯工业平均指数下跌 0.46%，标普 500 指数上涨 0.31%，
科技成长股明显跑赢，三大指数表现分化。债市方面，2 年期和 10 年期美债收益率分别
上行 4.9bp 和 2.4bp，收益率曲线熊平，或反映市场对美联储降息路径重新定价，短端
利率上行压力更大。与此同时，美元指数上涨 0.68%，COMEX 黄金下跌 0.83%。整体
来看，10 月美国劳动力市场供需两端均有所改善，新增就业明显回升，失业率下降，劳
动参与率同步上升，显示就业市场短期修复力度有所增强。但就业增长仍集中于少数行
业，长期失业压力上升、非自愿兼职增加，表明就业市场尚未实现全面、均衡的改善。

投资建议的评级标准  评级 说明 
评级标准为报告发布日后的 6 个月内公司股价（或行业
指数）相对同期基准指数的相对市场表现。其中 A 股市
场以沪深 300 指数为基准；新三板市场以三板成指（针
对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针对做市转让标的）
为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准。 
股票评级 
买入 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 10%以上 
增持 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 5%~10%之间 
中性 相对同期基准指数涨幅在-5%~+5%之间 
减持 相对同期基准指数跌幅在 5%以上 
行业评级 
看好 相对表现优于市场 
中性 相对表现与市场持平 
看淡 相对表现弱于市场

115年 9月全國賦稅收入初步統計 
一、全國賦稅收入 
（一）本月數 
9 月實徵淨額 4,965 億元，較上年同月增加 813 億元(+19.6%)。 
（二）累計數 
累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 3 兆 5,714 億元，較上年同期增加 7,004 億
元(+24.4%)。 
（三）達成率 
1.中央政府總預算業於 9 月 18 日公布，稅課收入較原預算案提高
6,000 億元，併計地方政府及特種基金後，全國稅課收入較預算案
提高 5,852 億元，本次發布均改依法定數計算預算達成率(實徵淨
額占全年預算數)。 
2.累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額占累計分配預算數 115.0%，占全年預算數
78.8%。 
圖 1 全國賦稅收入及年增率

說明：115 年 5-7 月年增率跳動較大，係因 114 年所得稅結算申報及繳納期限延長至 6 月底所致。

二、主要稅目別變動
（一）9 月實徵淨額與上年同月比較，以營業稅增加 297 億元、營利事業
所得稅增加 254 億元及證券交易稅增加 222 億元較多。
（二）累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額與上年同期比較，以證券交易稅增加 3,077
億元、營利事業所得稅增加 1,853 億元及營業稅增加 1,203 億元較多。
圖 2 主要稅目別變化
(115 年 1-9 月)
1.營利事業所得稅
(1)9 月實徵淨額 2,007 億元，較上年同月增加 254 億元(+14.5%)，主
因暫繳稅款成長。
(2)累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 1 兆 28 億元，較上年同期增加 1,853 億元
(+22.7%)，主因結算申報自繳、暫繳稅款俱增及延分期稅款陸續
入帳。
2.綜合所得稅
9 月實徵淨額 490 億元，較上年同月增加 34 億元(+7.4%)，主因扣繳
稅款增加所致；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 8,279 億元，較上年同期增加
790 億元(+10.6%)，主因結算申報自繳增加，以及盈餘分配、薪資所

得等扣繳稅款均呈成長。
3.營業稅
9月實徵淨額1,414億元，較上年同月增加297億元(+26.6%)，累計1至
9月實徵淨額6,230億元，較上年同期增加1,203億元(+23.9%)，均因內
需穩定，加以電子零組件、資通與視聽產品等進口代徵稅額增加，
惟出口暢旺，適用外銷零稅率營業人申請退稅亦增，抵銷部分增幅。

### Page 3

3
4.貨物稅
(1)9月實徵淨額100億元，較上年同月減少11億元(-10.0%)，主因汽柴
油減徵幅度擴大，惟電動車大量到港推升進口車稅額，抵銷部分
減幅。
(2)累計1至9月實徵淨額874億元，較上年同期減少207億元(-19.2%)，
除汽柴油降稅因素外，亦與新購汽機車及電動車之減免徵退稅增
加有關。
5.證券交易稅
(1)9 月及累計 1 至 9 月上市上櫃股票平均每日成交值為 1 兆 317 億
元、1 兆 1,461 億元，分別較上年同期(6,006 億元、4,451 億元)增
71.8%、1.6 倍。
(2)9 月 證 交 稅 實 徵 淨 額 534 億 元 ， 較 上 年 同 月 增 加 222 億 元
(+71.2%)；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 5,060 億元，較上年同期增加
3,077 億元(+1.6 倍)。
6.關稅
9 月實徵淨額 157 億元，較上年同月增加 3 億元(+1.9%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 1,224 億元，較上年同期增加 80 億元(+7.0%)，均因機械

及電機設備、基本金屬及其製品等進口稅額增加。
7.贈與稅
9 月實徵淨額 23 億元，較上年同月增加 6 億元(+35.7%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 211 億元，較上年同期增加 43 億元(+25.3%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加。
8.土地增值稅
9 月實徵淨額 56 億元，較上年同月增加 12 億元(+26.6%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 504 億元，較上年同期減少
14 億元(-2.8%)，主因房地交易量及大額稅款案件減少。
9.印花稅
9 月實徵淨額 31 億元，較上年同月增加 4 億元(+16.2%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 175 億元，較上年同期增加 15 億元(+9.0%)，主因銀行放
款稅額成長。
（三）前 9月主要稅目實徵淨額占分配預算數之比率：
1.大於 100%者有證交稅(272%)、遺產稅(155%)、贈與稅(132%)、印花
稅(130%)、營業稅(116%)、關稅(114%)、契稅(111%)、房屋稅(106%)、

綜所稅(101%)、牌照稅(101%)及營所稅(101%)等。
2.低於 100%者有貨物稅(98%)、菸酒稅(97%)、土增稅(85%)、地價稅
(84%)及特銷稅(57%)等。

三、各級政府賦稅收入概況
(一)中央政府
累計 1 至 9 月中央政府賦稅收入(不含中央統籌分配稅款)實徵淨額
2 兆 3,603 億元(占分配預算數 119.3%)，較上年同期增加 2,553 億元
(+12.1%)，雖新版財政收支劃分法開始施行，營業稅劃歸中央政府比
率大幅調降，減少 2,748 億元(-90.7%)，惟證交稅、所得稅分別增加 3,077
億元、2,230 億元，互抵後呈現上升。
(二)地方政府
累計 1 至 9 月地方政府賦稅收
入(含中央統籌分配稅款)實徵
淨額 1 兆 898 億元(占分配預
算數 109.4%)，較上年同期增
加 4,527 億元(+71.0%)，主因
納入中央統籌分配之營業稅
增加 4,030 億元(+2.1 倍)所致。
(三)中央特種基金
累計 1 至 9 月撥入中央特種基金之健康福利捐實徵淨額 195 億元，較
上年同期增加 2 億元(+0.8%)；房地合一課徵所得稅撥入住宅基金 135
億元，較上年同期減少 7 億元(-4.7%)；撥入長照基金 883 億元(其中 541

億元來自房地合一課徵所得稅、195 億元來自菸稅，146 億元來自遺贈
稅)，較上年同期增加 8 億元(+0.9%)。

## Document 088: 2026年9月消費統計

Report source: DIR

日本

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9番 1号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー 
このレポートは投資勧誘を意図して提供するものではありません。このレポートの掲載情報は信頼できると考えられる情報源から作成しておりますが、その正確性、完全
性を保証するものではありません。また、記載された意見や予測等は作成時点のものであり今後予告なく変更されることがあります。㈱大和総研と大和証券㈱は、㈱大和
証券グループ本社を親会社とする大和証券グループの会社です。内容に関する一切の権利は㈱大和総研にあります。無断での複製・転載・転送等はご遠慮ください。

2026年 11月 10日 全9頁

Indicators Update 
2026年 9月消費統計 
サービスを中心に消費全体が弱く、前月から減少

経済調査部 エコノミスト 龐 鈞文

[要約] 
◼ 2026年9月の家計調査における二人以上世帯の実質消費支出は前月比▲2.8％と3カ月
ぶりに減少した。複数の需要側統計を用いて補正した世帯消費動向指数（CTIミクロ）
で見ても実質消費は同▲1.6％と 2カ月連続で減少した。また、供給側統計の 1つであ
る商業動態統計では、CPI の財指数で実質化した小売販売額は同▲1.0％だった。総じ
て見れば、9月の個人消費は前月から減少したと判断される。 
◼ 先行きの個人消費は底堅く推移するとみている。名目賃金は最低賃金の引き上げもあ
って上昇が続く見込みである。一方、政府の物価高対策が予定されるものの、原油高
などによる幅広い分野への価格上昇の波及、円安や人件費上昇による価格転嫁などを
通じて個人消費を下押しする可能性には引き続き注意が必要だ。

＜2026年9月の消費総括＞サービスを中心に消費全体が弱い 
需要側統計である家計調査における二人以上世帯の実質消費支出は、前月比▲2.8％と3カ月
ぶりに減少した。複数の需要側統計を用いて補正した世帯消費動向指数（CTI ミクロ）で見て
も実質消費は同▲1.6％と 2カ月連続で減少した（図表 1）。 また、供給側統計の 1つである商
業動態統計では、CPIの財指数で実質化した小売販売額は同▲1.0％だった。総じて見れば、
2026年 9月の個人消費は前月から減少したと判断される。

＜CTIミクロ・家計調査（需要側）＞全ての費目が減少 
2026年 9月の CTIミクロ（二人以上の世帯）を費目別に見ると、10大費目1のうち、「教養娯
楽」（前月比▲3.3％）、「教育」（同▲6.5％）、「食料」（同▲0.9％）、「光熱・水道」（同▲
3.1％）、「家具・家事用品」（同▲4.8％）、「交通・通信」（同▲0.8％）、「保健医療」（同
▲2.4％）、「住居」（同▲1.6％）、「被服及び履物」（同▲3.0％）、「その他」（同▲0.4％）の全
ての費目が減少した（図表 2）。全ての費目が減少したのは消費税率が 10％に引き上げられた
2019年 10月以来だ。 
図表2：実質世帯消費動向指数（CTIミクロ）の前月比

（注）二人以上の世帯。総務省による季節調整値。シェアは 2025年の数値。 
（出所）総務省統計より大和総研作成

1 総務省による季節調整値。「その他」は、「食料」から「教養娯楽」までの合計を消費支出全体から減じて
算出している。

### Page 3

1

3 /9

続いて、CTIミクロの10大費目に含まれる個別品目への支出について確認する。「教養娯楽」
は2カ月連続で減少した。教養娯楽用品や教養娯楽耐久財を中心に支出が減少した。「教育」は
3カ月連続で減少した。授業料等を中心に支出が減少した2。「食料」は3カ月ぶりに減少した。
飲料を中心に、穀類などへの支出が減少した。「光熱・水道」は2カ月ぶりに減少した。電気代
を中心に、上下水道料などへの支出が減少した。「家具・家事用品」は2カ月連続で減少した。
家庭用耐久財を中心に、家事雑貨などへの支出が減少した。「交通・通信」は2カ月連続で減少
した。自動車等関係費や通信を中心に支出が減少した。「保健医療」は3カ月連続で減少した。
保健医療用品・器具を中心に支出が減少した。「住居」は2カ月ぶりに減少した。設備修繕・維
持を中心に支出が減少した。「被服及び履物」は2カ月連続で減少した。シャツ・セーター類や
他の被服を中心に支出が減少した。「その他」は2カ月連続で減少した。諸雑費を中心に支出が
減少した。 
消費支出を財・サービス別に見ると（大和総研による季節調整値、図表3中段右）、サービス
（前月比▲3.9％）が2カ月連続で減少し、全体を押し下げた。非耐久財（同▲1.6％）は2カ月
ぶりに、耐久財（同▲3.6％）は 5カ月連続で、半耐久財（同▲3.0％）は 2カ月連続で、いず
れも減少した。 
家計調査における基礎的支出は前月比▲0.6％と2カ月ぶりに減少した。また、選択的支出は
同▲7.6％と 3カ月ぶりに大幅に減少した（いずれも大和総研による季節調整値、図表 3中段
左）。

2 ただし、授業料等は子どもや学生がいる一部の世帯以外はほとんど支出しないため、サンプルサイズが小さ
く振れが大きい。

図表3：実質消費支出（季節調整値）

（注）二人以上の世帯。基礎的支出と選択的支出、財・サービス別支出は大和総研による季節調整値、それ以
外は総務省による季節調整値。「消費支出（除く住居等）」は、消費支出から「住居」「自動車等購入」「贈与

＜商業動態統計（供給側）＞小売販売額は名目・実質ともに 2カ月連続で減少 
2026年 9月の商業動態統計によると、名目小売販売額は前月比▲0.7％と 2カ月連続で減少
した（図表 4、5）。また、CPIの財指数で実質化した小売販売額は同▲1.0％だった。 
名目小売販売額を業種別に見ると、7業種中 4 業種が前月から減少した。「その他小売業」
（前月比▲2.1％）、「燃料小売業」（同▲2.7％）ともに 2カ月連続で減少した。「機械器具小売
業」（同▲2.3％）も 2カ月連続で減少した。家電大型専門店の販売額（大和総研による季節調
整値）（同▲3.2％）などが減少した。「飲食料品小売業」（同▲0.2％）は 2カ月連続で減少し
た。コンビニのファーストフード及び日配食品（同▲2.3％）と加工食品（同▲1.9％）が減少
した。 
他方、3業種が前月から増加した。「自動車小売業」（前月比＋0.1％）は 5カ月ぶりにプラス
に転じた。9月の新車販売台数（大和総研による季節調整値）は同＋2.0％と増加しており3、
この結果とも整合的だ。「織物・衣服・身の回り品小売業」（同＋4.2％）は2カ月ぶりに増加し
た。気温が低下したこと（巻末図表「全国の平均気温・日照時間・降水量」）や休日数が多か
ったことなどにより、秋物商品の販売が好調だったとみられる。「各種商品小売業」（同＋
2.1％）は 2カ月ぶりに増加した。スーパーの商品販売額（同＋1.0％）と百貨店の商品販売額
（同＋0.5％）が増加した。

7 /9

＜2026年10月の消費＞9月から減少を見込む 
業界統計や個社データ、JCB消費 NOWをもとに判断すると、2026年10月の消費は9月から減
少したとみている4。財消費では、10月前半の実績をもとに試算した家電のJCB消費額（大和総
研による季節調整値）、コンビニの JCB消費額（同）、新車販売台数（同）とアパレルの売上高
は減少した。一方、百貨店の売上高は堅調に推移した。サービス消費では、宿泊の JCB消費額
（同）は前月から減少した一方、外食のJCB消費額（同）は増加した。

＜先行き＞個人消費は底堅く推移する見込み／価格上昇の広がりに注意 
先行きの個人消費は底堅く推移するとみている。名目賃金の高い伸びが下支えとなるだろう。
ただし、原油高の波及や円安などによる物価上昇が実質賃金を押し下げ、消費の重荷となる可
能性には注意が必要だ。 
名目賃金の伸びは高水準で推移するだろう。厚生労働省によると、全ての都道府県で 10～12
月に最低賃金が引き上げられる。2026年度の最低賃金の全国加重平均額は1,177円で5、引き上
げ率は＋5.0％（前年度は＋6.3％）と高水準を維持した。また、企業収益の改善などを背景に、
高水準の年末賞与が期待される。 
消費者物価について、12月使用分から 2027年 2月使用分までの電気・ガス料金の補助とい
った政府の物価高対策が実施される予定だ6。ただし、原油高などにより、原材料費や輸送費の
上昇を通じて幅広い分野の価格に波及し、円安や人件費の高騰などによる価格転嫁も物価を押
し上げ、消費を下押しする可能性には引き続き注意が必要だ。また、帝国データバンクによる
と、飲食料品の 9～10月の大規模な値上げラッシュは一服したが、11月の値上げ品目数は前年
11月の約 10倍規模となり、引き続き高水準で推移している。賃上げや物流費、エネルギーコ
ストの上昇など、食品を取り巻くコスト環境が楽観視できる状況にはないため、値上げは続く
とみている7。 
また、金利上昇による住宅ローン等の利払い負担増が個人消費にもたらす悪影響にも警戒す
る必要がある8。さらに、消費動向調査（内閣府）によると、10 月の消費者態度指数（二人以
上の世帯、季節調整値）は 35.0（前月差▲0.4pt）と 2カ月連続で小幅に低下し、依然として
低水準にあり（巻末図表「消費者マインド」参照）、節約志向の高まりに引き続き注意が必要
だ。

4 詳細は、拙稿「消費データブック（2026/11/5号）」（大和総研レポート、2026年11月 5日）を参照。 
5 厚生労働省「全ての都道府県で地域別最低賃金の改定額が答申されました ～答申での全国加重平均額は昨
年度から 56円引上げの 1,177円～」（2026年9月3日） 
6 資源エネルギー庁「電気・ガス料金支援」（2026年11月9日閲覧） 
7 帝国データバンク「『食品主要 195社』価格改定動向調査 ― 2026年 11月 飲食料品値上げ、年間 2万品
目超え 11月は 1477品目 秋の『値上げラッシュ』は一服」（2026年10月 30日） 
8 詳細は、神田慶司・畑中宏仁・中村華奈子・横田凱「日本経済見通し：2026年8月」（大和総研レポート、 
2026年8月24日）を参照。

## Document 109: 261108_citi_MSI

Source PDF: all report pdf/261108_citi_MSI.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-08
Pages: 14
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
3Q26 Preview: Margins to Stay Elevated on Cost Pass-Through; Raise 
TP to NT$140, Stay Neutral
CITI'S TAKE 
MSI will report 3Q26 results in mid-Nov. We now expect net profit of 
NT$3.19bn and EPS of NT$3.77, 18% above con estimates, as 3Q26 GPM 
stays elevated at 15.8% vs. our previous 12.0% estimates, supported by 
better VGA supply and continued ASP pass-through of rising memory 
costs. For 4Q26E, we expect MB/VGA shipments to ease QoQ but ASPs to 
rise, with server contribution to further increase. Looking into 2027E, we 
expect PC demand to remain weak and pass-through room to narrow as 
memory price increases pace slows down. We raise 2026E-28E earnings by 
10-57% and lift our TP to NT$140 (from NT$118; 12x 2027E EPS). We stay 
Neutral, with likely limited upside given uncertain PC recovery and modest 
server contribution. 
3Q26 preview — MSI reported 3Q26 revenue of NT$56.9bn (+14% QoQ/-1% YoY), 
broadly in line with Citi’s forecast and slightly ahead of cons. While Sep MB and VGA 
shipments both exceeded expectations, as VGA supply improved. With memory and 
other raw material prices continuing to rise and MSI able to pass costs through, we 
expect 3Q26 GPM to stay elevated at 15.8%, well above our previous 12.0% estimate. 
Assuming only a modest increase in opex ratio, we forecast net profit of NT$3.19bn 
(+6% QoQ/+88% YoY) and EPS of NT$3.77, 18% above cons forecasts.
Price hike continues — We expect 4Q26E shipments to decline from 3Q26’s peak 
season. Mgmt. also indicated that Oct shipment could go down from Sep high base. 
However, we raise our 4Q26E pricing expectations and believe ASP should stay 
elevated, as memory prices keep climbing and MSI continues to pass costs through. 
The long-cultivated server business should also contribute higher than previous 
quarters. Looking into 2027, PC demand should remain weak as component costs 
stay high; we expect MSI’s MB/VGA/PC shipments to decline by double digits, with 
pass-through room narrowing as the price hike pace slows down.
Implications — We raise our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts by 10-57%, reflecting 
sustained strong component cost pass-through. We lift our TP to NT$140 (from 
NT$118), implying 12x 2027E EPS (rolled over from 12x 2026E EPS). We believe the 
share price already reflects MSI’s ability to pass costs through to end products, and 
we see limited upside, given that the PC demand recovery remains highly uncertain 
and the contribution from the new server business is still limited. Stay Neutral.

### Page 6

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Micro-Star
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 1998, Taiwan-based MSI is a leading
OBM/ODM for gaming PCs, motherboards, graphic cards, industrial PCs, and
servers. MSI started business as a motherboard manufacturer but
transformed itself to become a leading gaming PC play in 2012 with a
comprehensive product portfolio across the gaming system and components.
MSI enjoys about 16% global share in the gaming NB industry, and is ranked

No.3 in graphic card shipments and No.3 in motherboard shipments globally.
Investment strategy
We rate MSI shares at Neutral as we anticipate earnings upward pressure
from the delince in PC and MB/VGA shipments in the near-term. While the
soaring component costs could be partially offset by price hike, we expect
muted demand and AI resource crowding to wiegh on margins upside. Further
upside maintains limited, though, until we see a meaningful recovery in core

gaming demand or a more substantial contribution from the server business.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$140 is based on 12x 2027E EPS, below the stock's five-
year average forward 12-month PE. This valuation reflects the earnings
downside risks from muted PC demand and margin pressure from soaring
component costs, while accounting for the time required for the server
business to achieve meaningful scale.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause MSI's shares to trade above our target price

include: 1) a sharper-than-expected recovery in high-end gaming demand;
and 2) faster-than-anticipated revenue contribution from the AI server
segment. Key downside risks include: 1) a deeper-than-expected contraction
in global PC shipments; 2) prolonged supply shortages for gaming GPUs due
to Ai resource crowding; and 3) inability to pass through surging memory and
CPU costs to price-sensitive consumers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)

CPO: New growth opportunities from vertical integration — Hon Hai sees
significant long-term opportunities in both ASIC and vertical integration including
optical interconnect technologies. In optical networking, management believes the
transition from electrical to optical interconnects is structurally inevitable as AI
models expand and memory-bandwidth requirements increase. Hon Hai’s CPO

optical switching already starts mass volume production for its major customer,
and its micron-level precision manufacturing expertise in optical alignment,
connectors, and electrical-to-optical conversion could create additional value
through component integration and higher system-level content. Its extensive
engineering resources, ODM/JDM development capabilities, and vertically
integrated manufacturing platform should strengthen its competitive positioning.

Management expressed strong confidence in optical growth opportunities but did
not provide specific 2027 revenue guidance.

Robust financials and earnings growth, maintain Buy — Thanks to strong
demand from major CSP clients and its continuous automation and vertical
integration development, Hon Hai’s capex increased 43% YTD. Nevertheless, its
EBITDA increased 50.3% YoY to NT$369.2bn. To improve capital efficiency, Hon

Hai plans to expand consignment arrangements, optimize inventory and
receivables management, and carefully evaluate investment payback periods.
Management reiterated its long-term operating margin floor of above 3%, with
further potential upside from scale economies, automation, product mix
improvements, and greater vertical integration.
Given Hon Hai’s solid result and better operational leverage, we raise our
2026/2027/2028 EPS by 4%/5%/5%. Considering its solid execution, stable

dividend yield and attractive valuation, we reiterate our Buy rating with TP NT$400
unchanged. Our TP implies 15x of our 2027 EPS projection.

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
12 November 2026 Citi Research
9
Hon Hai Precision
Company description
Established in 1974, Hon Hai has become the largest EMS player in the world
in terms of sales. Through its vertical integration model or "eCMMS" (e-
enabled Components, Modules, Moves and Services), it provides
components, modules and system assembly products to many global OEMs
in the PC, communication and consumer sectors.
Investment strategy
We rate Hon Hai shares as Buy as we believe the company’s margins will

continue to improve, backed by a renewed focus on profitability, operational
efficiency and new businesses development. We think there’s more upside to
go from current level, as the underlying drivers are not just about product
cycle, but also about fundamental transformation and new business
opportunities.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on 15x average 2026E-27E EPS, which is
at the higher end of the historical trading range. We believe this multiple is

justified by the company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook,
as well as new endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon
Hai has been able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus we believe
a P/E valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand; 2) intensified peer
competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress;

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

Lotes Co. Ltd  | Asia Pacific
3Q GM Decline Eases; 4Q
Recovery in Focus
3Q results:
• GM came in at 44.8% (-60bps q/q, -640bps y/y), 20bps above our estimate.
3Q GM continued to face margin headwinds from rising raw material costs,
which we highlighted in our recent upgrade report here. We look forward to
management's comment during its results call to see if 4Q margin will be
guided to return to growth.
• Opex was  higher than expected at NT$2.2bn, 10% above our estimate and

9% above the Street.
• So OpM wa only 22.9% (+100bps q/q, -700bps y/y), 180bps below our
estimate and 250bps below the Street.
• Net profit was NT$1,919mn (-3% q/q, -17% y/y) for EPS of NT$17.09, 12%
below our estimate and 12% below the Street.
• Lotes is holding an earnings call on November 13 at 4 PM HKT; we will
provide takeaways after the call.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lotes Co. Ltd. (3533.TW)
Primary - Residual Income
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.6% (beta of
1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7% and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of
12.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected growth in the server business, such as from cloud and enter-
prise in China

n Stronger-than-expected DT demand
n Sooner-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n Faster ramp-up of its EV business
Risks to Downside
n Further deceleration of server demand from US hyperscalers
n Weaker-than-expected DT demand
n Slower-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n More severe pricing erosion driven by competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

◼ 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등에 주목

투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 50만원 하향(-6%) 
현대차 TP 50만원(시총 102조원)은 12MF EPS 41,978원에 Target PER 12배
적용하여 산출. 제한적인 실적 기대 반영해 타겟 밸류 하향함에 따라 TP 조정  
우려 대비 양호한 3Q26 실적으로 주가 하방 부담 완화. 주가의 추세적 상승 
위해서는 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등 확인돼야 할 
것. 기저효과에 따른 물량 개선이 주가 저점 받쳐줄 것이나, 현재 밸류(2027년 
PER 9.1배vs 글로벌 피어 6.1배)에서 추세 상승 이끌긴 어려울 것이기 때문. 
이에, 자동차 본업 외 1) 한/미국 Physical AI 투자 계획 및 2) BD 공급망 구축 
/양산 가시화에 따른 주가 반등 기대

3Q26 매출 45조(YoY -3%, 이하 YoY), OP 2.7조(+6%), OPM 5.9% 시현 
3Q26 OP 추정(2.5조)/컨센(2.4조) 상회. 파업에 따른 생산/판매 차질 영향으로 
기대 제한적이었으나, 비용 절감/환율 영향으로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현. 
[자동차] 매출 33조(-10%), OP 2.1조(+25%), OPM 6.5%. 도매 판매 92.1만대
(-11%). 판매 부진과 원자재 부담에도 불구, 환효과(+1.3조)/비용절감(+0.5조)
로 실적 방어. 이외 YoY 변수별 영향은 물량(-1.1조)/믹스(-0.6조)/원자재(-
0.5조). [금융] 매출 9.2조(+28%), OP 4,710억(-18%), OPM 5.1%, [기타] 매출 
2.9조(+2%), OP 712억(-72%), OPM 2.5% 기록

2027년 매출 191조(YoY +2%, 이하 YoY), OP 12.7조(+15%), OPM 6.6% 전망 
기저효과 바탕으로 연결 도매 물량 YoY +5% 증가 예상하나, 원달러 환율 -
4% 하락(가정 환율 1,380원)하며 1H27 → 2H27로가며 이익체력 개선 예상

### Page 5

DAISHIN SECURITIES

5  
  현대차 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스콜 QnA 
Q. 금융부문 환율 변동에 따른 4Q26 매출 및 손익 영향 
- 금융부문 환율 영향은 현대차 실적에 미치는 환율 영향과 동일한 방향으로 발생

Q. 3Q26 금융부문 수익성 악화 요인 
- 장기금리 상승에 따른 조달 및 이자비용 증가가 주요인. 자산/부채 만기 매칭에 따른 
금리 상승 영향 반영 
- EV 중심 중고차 가격지수 소폭 하락 및 차량 반환에 따른 매각손실, 금리 상승에 따
른 연체율 및 신용비용 상승 
- 차량 판매가격 인하 영향은 없으며, 3Q26 도매 판매 감소에 따른 금융 영업수익 영향 
불가피했음

Q. 향후 주주환원 정책에서 배당 및 자사주 매입 우선순위 
- 기존 밸류업 정책: 최소 DPS 1만원/TPR 35% 제시. 연간 배당 약 2.6조원 → TPR 
35% 충족 위한 잔여분 자사주 매입/소각 활용 
- 경영환경 변화 고려하여 기존 밸류업 프로그램 전반의 주주가치 제고 방안 재검토중 
- 새로운 정책 확정 및 주주 소통 전까지 기존 약속인 최소 DPS 1만원 유지할 계획 
- 시장의 배당 및 자사주 매입 간 균형 요구를 인지하고 있으며, 주주 의견 수렴 후 주
주가치 제고 계획 공유 예정

Q. 3Q26 영업이익 증감 요인 중 기타 +6,400억 발생 원인 
- 월별 컨틴전시 활동을 통한 비용 최적화 및 재료비 절감 지속 추진 
- 4Q26 원가 절감 효과는 3Q26 대비 확대 예상

Q. 3Q26 영업외손익 감소 원인 
- 3Q26 환율 급락으로 외화 매출채권 등 외화자산 관련 외화환산손실 및 외환거래손실 
발생 
- 매출채권 인식 시점과 회수 시점 간 환율 차이에 따른 외환차손 발생 
- 지분법 대상 기업들의 3Q26 실적 부진에 따른 지분법손익 감소도 일부 영향. 대부분 
외환 관련 손실에서 발생

Q. 중국 OEM 국내 진출 확대에 따른 대응 및 신차/가격 전략 
- 중국 OEM 및 테슬라의 국내 M/S 확대 대응중. 다만, 잦은 가격 조정은 중고차 잔존
가치 및 고객가치 훼손할 수 있어 지양 
- 현대캐피탈의 캡티브 금융 경쟁력 활용. 잔존가치 유예할부 등을 통해 월 납입금을 낮
춰 중국 OEM 대비 경쟁력 확보 
- 서비스 네트워크 기반으로 경쟁사 대비 경쟁력 확보 노력 
- 국내 신차: 4Q26 아반떼 HEV, 투싼, GV90, G80 HEV, 싼타페 FL(2026.12월), G90 
FL 등 출시 예정 
- 북미: 4Q26 투싼, GV80 HEV 출시 및 27년 초 GV90 출시 예상 
- 유럽: 아이오닉3 판매 개시, 4Q26 투싼/베이온 등 주요 모델 출시 예정 
- 중국: 아이오닉 V / 인도: 베이온, 아이오닉 P 등 출시 예정 
- 신차 출시 계획 연도별로 다수 예정. 가격은 원가/경쟁사/소비자 가격 수용성 등을 고
려해 결정

- 4Q26 법인세 수준은 QoQ 큰 변동 없을 것으로 전망

## Document 308: CNB Minutes_ Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline

Source PDF: all report pdf/CNB Minutes_ Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The Bank Board increased interest rates to 4% in a close call,
and we see valid arguments for both positions. Second-round
eﬀects will push inﬂation above the upper bound of the target
corridor for some time, while dwindling economic activity
warrants lots of caution. We see neither hikes nor cuts as the
most likely outcome for quite some time
David Havrlant
The Czech National Bank's decision to raise rates on 5 November was a close
call
Assessing the impact of Hormuz

The Czech National Bank increased the policy rate by 25bp to 4% in
a split vote on 5 November, with Governor Ales Michl and board
members Jan Kubicek, Jan Prochazka and Jakub Seidler voting for
a hike. Meanwhile, Deputy Governors Eva Zamrazilova and Jan Frait,
along with board member Karina Kubelkova, favoured interest rate
stability. We assess the current situation and the risks to the
outlook as complex and see relevant justiﬁcation for both options,

depending on whether your concerns are more on the side of
Your privacy is important

You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.
Essential

Targeting Cookies
Save preferences Accept all

### Page 2

second-round inﬂationary eﬀects or on the side of disappointing
economic activity and the consequences for consumption.
The Bank Board assessed overall risks as pro-inﬂationary. There
was an agreement that the secondary impacts of the energy shock
have not been signiﬁcantly reﬂected in domestic inﬂation so far.
However, as the Hormuz conﬂict becomes more protracted, the
likelihood of tangible second-round eﬀects increases. Jakub Seidler
noted that global energy prices have been elevated for too long, so

it would be diﬃcult to avoid a pass-through to other segments of
consumer prices. Deputy Governor Zamrazilova emphasised that
much depends on further developments in the Middle Eastern
conﬂict. Should tensions ease in the near future, a rate hike at the
November meeting would not be necessary. The key question is
whether the conﬂict will eventually pass or become the new
normal, with the latter implying persistently high energy costs.
Deputy Governor Frait stated that energy prices remaining at these

levels for such an extended period could have a negative impact on
demand and economic activity, mainly by reducing the real
incomes of households and companies. The current negative
supply shock has hit the Czech economy from a favourable starting
position, but the factors that helped keep inﬂation close to the
target this year are about to fade. The increase in longer-term rates
contributes to the restrictive stance of monetary policy, and implies

some softening in domestic lending activity. The board members
agreed that this is a key factor reducing the need for further
tightening for the time being.
To rise or not to rise?
However, as Jan Kubicek noted, core inﬂation is not slowing down
as previously assumed and is even slightly increasing. This
represents a fundamental concern, especially in combination with
the disappointing real growth performance. That said, producer
prices have embarked on an upward trend and may put further

pressure on the core rate. Jakub Seidler and Karina Kubelkova
emphasised that the renewed price increases in the energy and
food segment are usually the key drivers when it comes to inﬂation
Your privacy is important

our Cookie policy.
Essential
Performance Cookies
Targeting Cookies

### Page 3

expectations, and the ﬁrst signs of an increase are already visible.
And we all know that unanchored inﬂation expectations are one of
the things that keep central bankers awake at night.
Producer prices will drive consumer prices
Source: CZSO, ING, Macrobond
En gros, among the main reasons for a rate hike in November were:
i) persistent core inﬂation, ii) still robust wage dynamics and iii)
second-round eﬀects expected to bite soon. Among the main

reasons for staying on hold and evaluating were: i) secondary
inﬂationary impacts are so far limited, ii) long-term rates deliver
restriction, iii) disappointing economic activity.
We see the arguments in both directions as valid, which made the
November decision relatively complex and was reﬂected in the
close vote. Time will tell whether the second-round eﬀects that we
believe are just kicking in will prove signiﬁcant without weighing too
heavily on economic activity. Or whether the deteriorating real

purchasing power and dwindling real wage growth could lead to
weaker household consumption, which would in turn put
downward pressure on inﬂation. Both eﬀects are certainly
interconnected, which only underscores the beauty of
macroeconomics, and the formidable power of prices to clear the
market.

### Page 4

Punchy inﬂation across price segments
When it comes to our inﬂation forecast, we deﬁnitely see pro-
inﬂationary pressures when looking ahead. We expect annual
producer price growth to peak at around 7% in industry early next
year and at around 12% in agriculture mid-next year. At the same
time, the annual pace of import prices already quickened to above
8% in September. Those are not low growth rates, and all are going
to feed into consumer prices sooner or later. At the same time, the

koruna continues to weaken against the dollar, making imports
pricier. With all that on board, we see the yearly average of inﬂation
picking up from 2.1% this year to 3.2% next, reaching a peak of just
below 4% in February.
Inﬂation to hover above the target for
longer
Source: CNB, ING, Macrobond
The most pronounced uncertainty lies with January’s gain in
regulated prices. We have already collected information from
distributors and factor in a 3.5% increase in electricity prices, a

6.5% increase in natural gas prices, an 8% increase in heating
prices and a 6% rise in water charges. Nonetheless, not all
distributors will apply the same increase and not all households will
be aﬀected equally. Based on our best estimate, we expect a 3%
monthly increase in regulated prices in January, resulting in a 3.8%

### Page 5

year-on-year gain. However, should global oil and European natural
gas prices remain elevated, we expect further increases throughout
the year, lifting average regulated price growth to 4.5% next year.
This ﬁgure is still somewhat below the CNB's 5.6% forecast, which
seems to be at the upper bound of what we currently deem
plausible.
Regulated and food prices turn pro-
inﬂationary
Source: CNB, ING, Macrobond
Food prices are set to shift from an anti-inﬂationary position this

year to an inﬂationary one, averaging just below 3% next year, but
peaking at around 4%. That said, we sense quite some upward risks
here, especially should energy prices stay elevated for longer, which
would propel fertiliser prices and transport costs.
Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline
When it comes to core inﬂation, two opposing forces are set for a
duel: second-round eﬀects, coupled with rising import prices,
against a softening housing market and crumbling real purchasing

power. Timing is of the essence. We expect the former to strike ﬁrst,
but the latter to respond without much hesitation. For now, we see
the forces as roughly balanced, keeping average core inﬂation just
below 3% next year.

### Page 6

Consumption could slow tangibly along with
real wages
Source: CZSO, ING, Macrobond
So yes, upbeat inﬂation across price segments warrants restrictive
monetary policy. Meanwhile, weakening economic activity
warrants a lot of caution when it comes to any future rate hikes.
The thing is, we see real GDP growth averaging just 1.7% this year
and lifting oﬀ only gradually to 2.1% next year. Meanwhile, we see
household consumption slowing tangibly to 1.8% next year from

2.5% this year. Indeed, we are likely positioned at the lower end of
the forecast range, yet we take the view that a slowdown in real
wage growth will eventually take its toll. Therefore, we see neither
further hikes nor cuts when looking way ahead, with the 4% policy
rate settling in as the new normal.
The 4% rate makes itself at home

Source: CNB, ING, Macrobond
It would be a diﬀerent story, however, should we see another round
of escalation in the current conﬂicts, pushing Brent crude back

towards US$120/bbl. In that case, the path to a 4.5% CNB base rate
is pretty straightforward, if it pleases. If this were to happen, the
EUR/CZK would likely lend a helping hand to mitigate the imported
inﬂationary pressures from the eurozone, as the European Central
Bank seems constrained in this respect, not least by elevated French
bond yields. In contrast, should we slip into an episode of stagnant
growth in Europe for whatever reason, our hypothesis of a 4%

nominal interest rate becoming the new normal would start to look
a little shakier, at least temporarily.

## Document 375: NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications (2026-11-13)

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications (2026-11-13).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#labour #newzealand
NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications
By Kelly Eckhold
Chief Economist, Westpac New Zealand
13:16 November 04 2026 Share
The Q3 labour market statistics were weaker than expected. We discuss potential
implications for the RBNZ noting that a December hike remains more likely than not at this
point.
Q3 Labour market data: potential RBNZ implications
The Q3 labour market data were weaker than RBNZ and our expectations.

### Page 2

Today's labour market data seem unequivocally weaker than expected by either us or the RBNZ. While
likely not a gamechanger for the cycle overall, it will add ammunition to the arguments of the MPC
doves that a pause in interest rate rises will make sense soon - perhaps in December or in the New
Year. It’s likely the case that a bit much was priced into December, so the change in market
probabilities in the wake of today’s data makes sense - although my base case remains that a
December rate rise remains more likely than not.
The message from the RBNZ in the October meeting though was to be careful about pricing in "done
deals" and that the outlook will be data-dependent. As we noted at the time, alongside measures of
inflation expectations, the key domestic data to watch ahead of the RBNZ’s 9 December meeting will
be indicators of what is happening to excess capacity (the output gap) as this will be a key determinant
of the Bank’s assessment of the outlook for inflation over the coming year.
It’s useful to remember in the RBNZ’s forecasting framework that GDP figures are the main
determinant of excess capacity in the economy (i.e., the output gap). The Q2 GDP data told us that the
output gap probably narrowed a bit through Q2, mainly reflecting historical revisions. In light of today’s
labour market numbers, the forecast for growth in Q3 may have some downside risk - but there are
conflicting indicators there, so it’s not that clear. However, it’s probably hard to argue that the level of
excess capacity reduced further in Q3 given today’s data. 
In the eyes of dovish MPC members, the rise in the unemployment rate and other measures important
for the RBNZ's labour market dashboard likely push in the direction of having greater comfort with the
medium-term inflation outlook. The vacancy to unemployment ratio increased a bit, labour constraints
eased a bit, and the unemployment rate has increased. Hence from a labour market perspective it
could be argued that policy could have more time all else equal. The outcome of the election, with the
possibility of a new RBNZ mandate, could increase the prominence of these factors - although this
won’t likely be an explicit factor in the RBNZ’s thinking in December even though it may be in the minds
of individual MPC members.
It could be the case that the argument for increases in the OCR track through 2027 could be
undermined with this data. It’s important to note (probably self-evidently) that there are other data that
will also be relevant. Hence the risk of a pause earlier in the New Year has probably increased with this
data, all else equal. I don't think our forecast peak OCR is really at issue here, but the time taken to get
there could be more flexible. Takes along the lines of "3% is the terminal rate" are very premature and
misplaced, in our view.

A December hike remains more likely than not, but there will be a clear debate to be had between the
MPC hawks and doves.
The forecast peak OCR is likely less impacted than the time to reach that peak. Further pauses in the
tightening cycle could come earlier than currently forecast. 
The outlook for the exchange rate is especially clouded by this data should it take a longer time for
NZ rates to move closer to global norms. 
Watch for updated RBNZ commentary on 10 and 19 November. The Governor’s observation that it
would have been useful to update markets ahead of the October meeting could imply a greater
willingness to comment in the coming weeks.

### Page 3

The outlook for the exchange rate is undermined by this data. I feel very happy about our year-end
target of 79c for the NZD/AUD rate with the risk that a recovery in the NZD takes longer than currently
assumed.
Looking ahead, there are some opportunities for the RBNZ to update markets on their views. Top of
mind is Paul Conway's 10 November speech to the UBS Australasia Conference. The Governor is also
speaking to the Institute of Directors on 19 November. If the RBNZ has something to say, then it might
happen in conjunction with these events (the 10 November speech is titled “How is the NZ economy
tracking”, so it will be difficult for Conway to avoid saying something about how he is interpreting
today’s data). We also note the Governor's comments in the October MPR that it could have been
useful to have been more flexible about updating markets on emerging views of new economic data.
This might increase the likelihood that these events could be used to provide guidance on the RBNZ’s
views of this data (and other emerging events), especially if it feels that the market’s interpretation is at
odds with its own.

Media contact
Kelly Eckhold
Chief Economist
+64 9 348 9382
 +64 21 786 758 
 kelly.eckhold@westpac.co.nz

## Document 391: Sales Trader Commentary_ Squeezing the Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City _ GSTW

Source PDF: all report pdf/Sales Trader Commentary_ Squeezing the Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City _ GSTW.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Sales Trader Commentary: Squeezing the
Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX
Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City | GSTW
12 November 2026 | 3:15 PM Taiwan Standard Time
Robert Wang
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. Taipei Branch
FICC & Equities
Post the historical open on 11/6/2026, we saw a jaw-dropping 14% climb from day-
low last Friday. Finally took a breather (and a big one too) today; admittedly, I was
writing a different narrative, but last hour fade in Taiwan has forced me to rewrite this

(>1k points drop and picked up market turnover). So, where do we go from here?
Check out below for my observation and investor feedback.
Key Investor Feedback and Takeaway
Sentiment. Sentiment is still cautious among foreigners; a lot of HFs faced serious
short squeeze from this rally. Yet, many are still reluctant to cover all their short
positions given the new short sell restrictions. and hence held on to the pain trades.
Interestingly, market still exhaust short sell quota almost every day. Additionally,

there is little conviction for a full-on rally and also low willingness to chase
around here given year-end. Overall, HFs have de-grossed much, one of the most
aggressive de-gross per GS prime book data. Key focus will be whether LOs are
willing to participate and to what extent will they add back the positions. Only
then, we will likely see a more sustainable rally further from here. Bottom line: are
we out of the woods? Not yet. Cautious optimism is perhaps the most positive

thing I can say for now.
Positioning. Foreigners have net sold for 10 straight sessions, totaling around
US$25.4bn. Most outflows are in TSMC but worth noting that we finally saw TSMC
net buy for the first time since 2 weeks yesterday. On the other hand, local funds
have been good size net buyers to absorb the foreign outflows, mainly from retail's
mutual fund subscription. Just regurgitating some of my berg blurbs from the past
few days, we still see lots of short interest in crowded long sectors, most in

ABF/CCL (i.e. NY Plastics) followed by CPO names; and most smid cap
outperformance was driven by short covering. The overly sold names saw little
attention and minimum desire to bottom fish (i.e. Delta, Chroma, KYEC). On a
brighter note, we also picked up some real money inflows into TSMC this week, but
I don't know whether it's enough to turn the tide. The more convicted long right
now for foreigners is still ASE in my opinion.
Retail. This is perhaps the most important factor now in Taiwan. Coupled with the

sentiment boost from National Stabilization Fund, many retail decided to hold
instead of cutting loss and even bought into the dip. For those retail that were
facing margin call during this correction, some were eventually saved by the market
rally. Cleaner positioning for sure, though perhaps not clean enough to fuel another
11/12/26, 3:20 PM Sales Trader Commentary: Squeezing the Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City | GSTW

https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/dc88e4dd-47de-45c1-bbf3-31c2c5c57299 1/11

### Page 2

10% climb in index. Per my local investor feedback, the smart pundits actually
many have missed out on this rally (of course they've also de-grossed much ahead).
Margin loan balance and maintenance ratio are two key data that I will follow
closely in the coming weeks.
Fundamentals. Fundamentals are still good with most company reporting record
high quarterly earnings. Monthly revenue also grew MoM. But stock trading is all
about forward looking. People are anticipating AI capex growth to slow down or

even plateau in the coming 2 years. Quanta (2382 TT) earnings call yesterday
validated this narrative - still high growth in 2027 but unlikely to see another triple-
digits rapid growth like 2026. Earnings outlooks probably matter less than
positioning and macro backdrop. With that, we will conclude most of our 3Q26
earnings this week with Hon Hai (2317 TT) as the main finale.
Liquidity Event: MSCI just published their quarterly review, and NYPCB (8046 TT)

and TUC (6274 TT) will be added into the index, while Lotes (3533 TT), Chailease
(5871 TT), Wan Hai (2615 TT), an E-Ink (8069 TT) will be removed. Kinsus (3189
TT) did not get added due to its disposition status. See below and this report for
our flows estimate.
Risk. Risk reward level here is not attractive, especially when the rebound came so
aggressively. Momentum to sustain the rally is not constructive, so investors should
be cautious of more volatility and further unwind (if there's anything to be

unwound). Jensen and Nvidia's headlines are certainly not helping, though I did see
some funny memes on the social media. Credit is another risk here; neoclouds top
line is +ve but let's not forget at what cost are these companies carving those
revenue from. Otherwise, market seems to process the macro prints well, with now
expecting a rate hike in Dec'26 and Mar'27. Last but not least, cross-strait tension
can be a focus next during Trump-Xi meeting at the APEC Summit next week. I

know the Taiwanese has been (probably will be) indifferent and just another BAU,
but anything assertively negative comment on Taiwan might fret the macro
traders, such as Xi's comment just now.
My View. Not a BEAR so far. I do not think the market is broken, but I
acknowledge the downside risk has risen again and enlarged as I respect the
technicals. That said, I am still excited about next week, even though we have fewer
fundamental catalysts now. In the US, Nvidia is set to report next week. A lighter

book might fare better in today's environment. To end on a fun personal note, GTA
VI will finally come out after 13 years of waiting. So if you can't find me on
Bloomberg, try looking for me in the Vice City.

## Document 410: Three Questions About China’s Hidden Foreign Exchange Assets (2026-11-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Three Questions About China’s Hidden Foreign Exchange Assets (2026-11-12).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 17
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
Three Questions About China’s Hidden Foreign
Exchange Assets
A trillion-dollar gap in foreign exchange purchases raises questions about where
China’s foreign currency assets are held.
Carlos Rawlins/Reuters
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
PUBLISHED
November 11, 2026 8:32 p.m.
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
The PBOC argues—unpersuasively, I think—that it
doesn’t have any hidden foreign exchange reserves. I
would reframe that as hidden foreign currency assets,
as foreign currency lent out (or swapped out) with
domestic financial institutions is a classic way of
masking reserves.
Many in Asia—and some in the IMF—are inclined
to believe the PBOC. Fair enough, it is hard to

challenge an institution as large and powerful (in
some ways) as the PBOC.

### Page 3

3
But to convince, the PBOC really needs to provide
answers to three questions.
1. What explains the current gap between foreign
exchange settlement and the PBOC’s reserves?
Settlement is the purchase of foreign exchange in the
market by the PBOC and the state banks. It
technically implies taking an open foreign currency
position, as the foreign exchange is being bought with
renminbi.
Historically, it correlated with changes in the PBOC’s
balance sheet.

And some gaps before 2012 can be explained. In late
2005 and 2006, the PBOC did over $100 billion in
swaps with the state banks, who used the FX to buy
foreign bonds (portfolio debt outflows surged). Those

### Page 4

4
swaps were unwound after the global financial crisis,
and actually provided some of the foreign currency
that “funded” the China Investment Corporation
when it initially scaled up.
And in 2007 and 2008, the PBOC required that the
state banks hold FX against their domestic RMB
deposits (to keep the FX out of reserves; it was held
on deposit at the PBOC and shows up as “other
foreign assets” on the PBOC’s balance sheet and as
“other, other, short-term” in the balance of payments.*

After 2018, though, there are unexplained gaps
between the FX bought in settlement and the
PBOC’s balance sheet (technically cumulative
monthly changes to capture the fact that FX is held as
its initial CNY purchase price).
There was a net accumulation of FX outside the
PBOC’s balance sheet in 2020 and 2021, when the
CNY was under pressure to appreciate. There are
modest sales in 2023 and 2024 when there is
depreciation pressure. And there are large purchases

in 2025 and the first 9 months of 2026—total
purchases in settlement in the last 12 months (spot)
are $500 to 600 billion, while the increase in the
PBOC’s balance sheet is only around $50 billion over
the same period.

### Page 5

5
The gap over time is now $1 trillion. The PBOC
needs to be able to answer a simple question: who
holds this FX, and who holds the associated FX
market risk (given the FX has been purchased with
RMB).
The monthly pattern of growth in settlement strongly
suggests it is backdoor intervention; there are sales
when spot is weak (economically) relative to the fix,
and purchases when spot is either at the fix or just
stronger than the fix (the strong edge of the band

hasn’t been used). The pattern of purchases and sales
thus looks exactly like that of a central bank
maintaining a tight band around a (crawling) peg.
If the PBOC cannot credibly explain this gap, there
isn’t much more to discuss. The PBOC argues that it
hasn’t swapped FX with the state banks or put foreign
currency on deposit at the state banks. Fair enough.

### Page 6

6
But someone is buying in settlement; the PBOC
ought to know who…
2. Missing interest income
There is an obvious tell here—Bloomberg reported
back in 2022 that the PBOC transferred $158 billion
in accumulated interest income to the Ministry of
Finance as part of a special dividend to hold the
budget deficit down. Again, fair enough. But
absolutely nothing changed in the FX reserves held
on the PBOC’s balance sheet or in the balance of
payments.
That highlights a broader issue. Since the end of

2016, the PBOC’s balance sheet FX hasn’t really
changed.

### Page 7

7
Peer institutions generally have an upward slope to
their reserves as a result of “gains from reserve
management” or “reinvestment of interest income.” **
The Central Bank of China (Taipei) and Japan’s
Ministry of Finance are good examples.
The interest on ~$3 trillion in reserves should be
around $100 billion a year. A bit less during the “low-
for-long” era before the pandemic, when SAFE wasn’t
getting much income out of its euro portfolio. A bit

more now, when U.S. short-term rates are well above 3
percent and SAFE’s legacy agencies carry yields of 5
or 6 percent. $100 billion over 10 years is real money
—roughly a trillion dollars.
If SAFE has steadily been selling its interest income,
it should say so (the fact that BOP reserves have often
diverged from the PBOC balance sheet suggests a
more complex story).

### Page 8

8
Moreover, if SAFE has been transferring the FX to
one of China’s many sovereign wealth funds
(including those controlled by SAFE) or the policy
banks, it should say so.
But SAFE cannot say what you see is what we really
have if it cannot explain how it handles the interest
on its now $3.5 trillion in reserves.
3. The accounting for the PBOC’s entrusted loans
and other foreign currency claims on domestic
financial institutions, notably the policy banks

FX handed over to a sovereign wealth fund to manage
doesn’t appear in reserves. It isn’t a liquid reserve asset.
But it should appear elsewhere in the balance of
payments as either portfolio debt or portfolio equity.
And FX handed over to the policy banks through
mechanisms such as entrusted loans (or a simple
deposit or swap) doesn’t count as a foreign reserve—
the foreign currency claim is on a domestic financial
institution, and that isn’t considered a “foreign” asset.

There is no doubt that the PBOC has domestic
claims on China’s financial institutions. The use of
“entrusted” loans to fund the policy banks in 2010
was reported by Caixin and the Financial Times.

### Page 9

9
$95 billion in entrusted loans were converted into
equity in the CDB and China Exim back in 2015
(giving SAFE majority equity stakes in both
institutions through its Buttonwood investment
vehicle).
And the SAFE’s 2020 annual report contained a ten-
year progress report on the “diversified use” of China’s
FX (basically, the non-reserve use) which indicated
that SAFE continued to provide entrusted loans to
small, medium, and large Chinese financial

institutions.
Now perhaps these instruments have been unwound
—the China Development Corporation (per Stephen
Green, a friend) has reduced its foreign currency
balance sheet over the last few years. But that should
lead the foreign currency to flow back to SAFE
absent another use—and that increase in FX isn’t
showing up in the PBOC’s reserves.
In other words, SAFE should provide a transparent
accounting of the FX that has been bought but
transferred to other financial institutions and any

ongoing foreign currency exposure to the domestic
banking system (including the policy banks).
These aren’t big asks. The settlement gap is big and
obvious, interest income is a known issue, and SAFE
itself has reported its use of entrusted loans in the
past. There is no issue around the past use of swaps

### Page 10

10
either. A substantial share of the banks’ foreign
currency assets are funded domestically. It should be
possible for SAFE to explain the role, if any, it has
played in this funding.
There is, of course, another issue around China’s
balance of payments: the growth in foreign currency
external assets funded out of domestic foreign
currency deposits.
China argues that the growing foreign assets of its
state banks are simply a natural part of a growing

domestic financial system. But there is more to the
story.
Onshore FX deposits have tended to rise when
economics say they should not, and fall when the
economics say they should (offshore FX deposits
behave as expected). In 2020 and 2021, FX deposits

### Page 11

11
rose even when there was more yield on RMB
deposits.
And RMB deposits have continued to rise in 2025
and 2026 even with the RMB’s appreciation
offsetting the higher dollar deposit rate. This isn’t an
accident: banks just raised the interest rate that they
pay on dollar deposits to encourage exporters NOT to
convert their export proceeds into RMB and thus
take pressure off the PBOC (per Reuters).
In other words, the pattern of the banks’ foreign

currency asset growth suggests that they are offsetting
appreciation pressure by bidding for FX (or receiving
it from other state actors), not that FX flows into the
banks during periods when onshore residents worry
about depreciation.

### Page 12

12
Right now, the “settlement gap” is the most pressing
issue. FX deposit growth in excess of FX loan growth
can explain FX outflows of around $150 billion in the
four quarters to Q3. The FX outflows implied by
settlement reached $600 billion over the last four
quarters. Together they account for the bulk of
China’s reported $825 billion plus (I estimate a Q4
2025 to Q3 2026 surplus of around $900 billion) in
outflows implied by the reported current account.***

### Page 13

13
It goes without saying that this matters because the
weak RMB is the central reason for China’s massive
export outperformance since 2023—and that export
outperformance has continued in 2026.****
* I have been doing this a long time; I actually do
know most of the historical quirks in China’s balance

### Page 14

14
of payments data.
** The MoF can count a portion of its unrepatriated
gains from accumulated interest on its large reserves
against the budget deficit, but since the actual
interest is held abroad, it still needs to borrow
domestically against those reserves (domestic JGB
issuance and foreign reserves both go up).
*** My estimate of a larger “true” current account
comes from moving gold imports over to the financial
account, and from an assumption that undercounting

of the reinvested earnings of funds held offshore
explains the obvious problem in the investment
income account.
**** The spike in imports in Q1 wasn’t entirely real.
China was slow to adjust its import price index for
the rise in semiconductor/chip prices. This inflated
both import and export volumes in Q1. The rise in
gold imports this year also registers in the import
volume series—though the rise is best understood as
“importing” a financial asset.

### Page 15

15
This work represents the views solely of the author(s). The
Council on Foreign Relations is an independent,
nonpartisan membership organization, think tank, and
publisher, and takes no institutional positions on matters
of policy.

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投

《高利高通膨壓抑，留意日股短線震盪加大》
10月日本股市大幅震盪修正，符合本行下調評等之
預期。其中，日經225指數在10月初一度上破7萬點
大館，此後因AI相關估值疑慮、科技股修正與長端利
率上行，全球科技股估值大幅修正，日經225指數在
10月23日盤中一度下探61,462點。
日本兩大指數在本月表現也呈現相對分化。日經225
權重權重集中於東京威力科創、愛德萬測試、軟銀集
團等半導體與AI標的，在本波修正幅度較大；相反
的，TOPIX指數因金融、內需與防禦類股權重較高，
加上利率下行有利銀行以外的內需類股，回檔相對溫
和。也間接反映了10月的修正偏向是「AI交易擁擠
的釋放」，而非全面性的景氣衰退定價。
債市方面，10年期日本公債殖利率在10月初一度升

至3.11%、創1999年以來新高，反映日本央行升
息、以及高市內閣財政擴張的雙重疑慮。
此後殖利率一路回落至10月26日的2.82%、下行約
23個基點。這一下行幅度反映全球AI相關信用風險
引發的避險需求。
重點摘要
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9，中國信託商業銀行整理
5
AI相關估值疑慮、科技股修正
• 10月日股受AI估值修正及長天
期利率波動影響而震盪下跌，
但修正主要集中於半導體與AI
權值股。
• TOPIX相對抗跌，顯示市場仍
未全面反映景氣衰退，而是資
金自擁擠交易部位撤出。

《日本出口維持韌性，內需動能出現疑慮》
經濟基本面部分，日本9月出口在半導體與AI相關需
求帶動下，連續13個月維持正成長，惟增速放緩至
16.7%、略低於預期；但進口年增卻升至18.9%，主
要因為進口能源價格大漲所致，導致貿易逆差擴大，
外需雖強卻未能轉化為淨出口貢獻。
內需與生產端則收高利高通膨壓力同步降溫。工業生
產增速自年中高點回落，製造業PMI降至七個月來最
低，整體擴張速度已低於上半年水準，細項中新訂單
與出口訂單成長同步放緩，能源與勞動成本則大幅走
升，對企業營運形成壓力。內需改善亦相當有限，零
售銷售下滑、失業率微升、求人求職比回落，反映勞
動市場略有降溫；所幸，實質薪資已連續八個月正成
長、消費者信心小幅回升。

整體而言，日本經濟仍處於「名目成長、實質停滯」
的格局，擴張主要仰賴出口，內需復甦力道偏弱，而
高油價帶來的逆差壓力與生產動能轉弱，將是後續成
長的主要考驗。基於地緣與油價的不確定性，10月
日本央行暫停升息，維持利率不變。
重點摘要
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理
5
貨幣政策正常化以及財政疑慮推升日債殖利率走高
• 日本出口持續受惠半導體與AI
需求，惟能源價格上漲推升進
口成本與貿易逆差。工業生產、
PMI及勞動市場同步降溫，經
濟仍維持「出口強、內需弱」
格局，成長動能面臨考驗。

《日銀暫停升息，12月升息預期支撐利率高檔》
日本央行已於9月會期將政策利率上調25個基點至
1.25%，為1995年以來最高 ，也是三個月內第二
次、1990年以來最快的升息節奏。
隨著近期經濟數據，10月份日央如預期暫停升息一
碼，會議同步公布《經濟與物價形勢展望報告》，根
據央行經濟預測顯示潛在通膨「已大致達到2%」。
配合植田和男在10月16日記者會曾表示，若基礎通
膨進一步上升將是「無法接受」的，並在10月19日
國會答詢時表示潛在通膨非常接近2%目標。
加以隨著國際油價再度上破110美元大關，短期政策
重點從「對抗通縮」轉為「防止通膨超標」，是以
12月再度升息一碼的機率已大幅提高。
另一方，高市內閣的食品消費稅減稅與國防支出增

加，使市場對財源安排存疑。不過，10月19日高市
在國會承諾在下一財年預算中妥善管控國債發行規
模，使近期長端利率有所回落。但12月下一財年預
算與國債發行計畫公布時，財政紀律的可信度將再受
檢驗，預期短線利率上行趨緩，維持高檔盤整。
重點摘要

《盤勢觀點-資金輪動加速，防禦成長族群接棒》
10月日本股市震盪、類股表現分化。市場資金明顯
由上半年漲幅龐大的AI硬體與半導體族群撤出，轉向
軟體服務、醫療保健及內需消費等防禦成長型產業。
其中，東京威力科創、SCREEN、瑞薩電子等指標股
普遍出現雙位數跌幅。相較之下，軟體服務類股大漲
17.9%，BayCurrent、SoftBank等個股表現突出。醫
藥生技、消費零售及娛樂觀光相關族群亦受惠於防禦
需求與內需消費韌性而表現相對亦強勢。值得注意的
是，銀行與非銀行金融股同步走弱，多家大型金融機
構跌幅介於8%至15%，顯示市場擔憂金融條件收緊
可能壓抑經濟活動與資本市場表現。
整體而言，10月日股市場風格由「AI硬體主導」轉

向「防禦成長與內需消費主導」，同時對經濟基本面
疑慮小幅升溫，8~9月相對強勢的金融出現回調壓
力。展望未來，短期盤勢仍受利匯率壓力，以及科技
股評價調整影響，加以1H27預估日經225指數企業
獲利年增率轉為負成長，因此本行維持9月以來下調
日股至中性的看法不變，待國際油價回落以及年底年
度預算案底定後，可伺機布局。
重點摘要
5
• 市場風格由AI硬體轉向軟體服
務、醫療保健及內需消費等防
禦成長族群。半導體、金融與
工業設備股表現疲弱，反映投
資人開始關注高利率環境及企
業獲利成長放緩風險。

《盤勢觀點-澳洲景氣放緩，股匯市轉向盤整》
隨著國際油價維持衝高，澳洲央行（RBA）於9月再
度升息將現金利率提高至4.6%，官員雖表示若通膨
壓力持續，不排除進一步緊縮。但在高利率與高通膨
壓力持續積累下對景氣負面壓力逐漸加大。
資料顯示，澳洲9月失業率升至4.72%，已連續兩個
月 落 在 澳 央 主 席 認 為 可 有 效 壓 抑 通 膨 的 失 業 率
4.5~5.0%區間；而通膨方面，9月份澳洲CPI雖由
4.0%升至4.3%，但核心CPI僅有2.2%，加以住房通
膨明顯回落，因此本行預期澳央轉向觀望、本輪升息
暫時告終，2027年政策轉向觀望。
外需方面，中國經濟基本面持續低迷，消費與投資不
振亦難以挹注澳洲出口動能，外需對澳幣支撐力量有

限，加以預期聯準會在12月再度升息，將呈現「利
差有撐但優勢遞減」格局，因此預估澳幣短線維持在
0.7~0.71區間盤整。
綜上，持續升息後澳洲基本面轉弱，但高油價壓力又
迫使央行貨幣政策難以轉向寬鬆，加以企業獲利未見
改善動能，澳洲股市難以樂觀，本行維持中性展望。
重點摘要
• 高油價迫使澳洲央行維持偏
鷹態度，但高利率已逐步壓
抑就業與內需。
• 中國需求偏弱限制出口前景，
加上美澳利差優勢收斂，預
期澳幣短期維持區間震盪，
澳股展望偏中性。

◼ 金融市場：半導體和AI相關類股受挫，日台韓
股受連帶影響，不過資金輪動至其他產業，使
美股指數整體穩健。美債殖利率走低、美元下
滑。
◼ 投資建議：若資料中心的建置時程受影響，將
影響相關業者的營收和償債時程，提高AI相關
類股的波動。但就趨勢來說，既有政策不會因
此出現重大轉折，穩健成長的企業獲利提供重
要支撐，AI類股修正時期資金仍在其他類股間
輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢
熱門議題/資產影響推估 市場焦點
➢ 美國國會變天：民主黨不僅重新奪回眾議院，也
以遠超預期的幅度拿下參議院多數席次。整體政
策方向其實不會發生重大改變，但民主黨未來可
能利用財政法案與債務上限談判，推動限制對伊
朗戰爭、部分撤銷關稅以及影響財政支出。
➢ 市場焦點放在資料中心建置進程：AI資料中心的
建設是否受到影響，是目前市場最關注的焦點。
本行認為短期逆風確實存在，但不影響長期建置
趨勢。

美國期中選舉國會變天
• 盤勢回顧-半導體和AI相關類股受挫 P3

• 產業影響-AI建設加強監管但不會喊停 P6-7
• 市場觀點-資金輪動而未明顯離場 P8-P10

美國國會變天，AI概念受挫然未見風險情緒，殖利率回落
AI概念受挫，資金輪動至其他板塊
盤勢回顧
美10年期利率回到5%以下，信用利差自高點收斂
3
• AI概念受挫，資金輪動至其他板塊：民主黨全面掌控國會後，市場擔憂AI產業、大型雲端服務與資料中心的監
管與審查將顯著收緊，使相關類股、日台韓股市均下挫，不過資金輪動到其他產業，支撐美股指數維持穩定。
• 財政紛擾仍存但支出展望中性，美10年債利率來到5%以下：市場開始反應財政政策可望趨於中性，再加上地
緣政治有望緩和，布蘭特原油跌破100美元/桶，支持美國公債利率持續走低至5%以下，自10月高點以來下滑
近50個基點。殖利率下滑輔以金融市場不確定因素降低支撐股市反彈，同步帶動美國信用債利差收斂，11月以
來美國信用債總報酬緩步走揚。

### Page 4

©  CTBC
兩黨對立將放大財政期限影響與紛擾，整體財政支出趨於中性
兩黨對立，財政期限的重要性上升
政策影響
2027年的財政動能預計將大致維持中性
4
• 財政期限的重要性上升，帶來政治紛擾：民主黨可利用具強制性的時間節點，作為政策讓步的槓桿。第一個要
注意的時間點是12/11政府支出授權到期，不過將期限延到2027年2至3月的機率較高(否則政府可能關門)。另
外推論1Q27將臨近債務上限，以目前的財政部存款和非常規措施的使用，最多可支應至2027年8月至10月。
• 財政政策整體趨於中性：分裂政府通常意味著較不具擴張性的財政政策，而眾議院掌握聯邦預算的撥款權，將
可能透過拒絕追加或直接限制戰爭預算的方式來迫使戰事降溫。然民主黨同時也可能與共和黨協議，以撤銷去
年大而美法案的支出削減措施、恢復健康保險補貼等作為交換條件，讓白宮得以增加國防預算。綜合來看，短
線面臨預算協調的紛擾，美債殖利率波動仍可能偏高，但趨勢上政策制衡效果增強，有利減輕殖利率的壓力。

關稅結構可能改變，資料中心監管程度提高但不影響建置趨勢
民主黨將嘗試撤回部分關稅，但整體稅率變化有限
政策影響
對資料中心監管程度將提高，但非禁止建置
5
• 民主黨將嘗試撤回部分關稅，但整體稅率變化可能有限：民主黨領袖已表示將嘗試撤銷已實施的部分關稅，但
川普政府仍可利用301條款推動新的關稅，整體有效關稅稅率變化可能有限，但其中可能的重點是降低對加拿大
的關稅。
• 對資料中心監管程度將提高，不影響建置趨勢：傳統共和黨票倉的德州變天，而德州是最大的資料中心建設地
區，新任的民主黨州長可能要求企業自行承擔電力及電網擴建成本、保障居民用水優先權，並在保護規範明確
之前影響資料中心的建設進度。不過本行認為，除了要求企業揭露資料來源與訓練資訊、建立安全治理架構，
以及落實其他風險管理措施等規定之外，尚無太多州級監管制度被認為具有高度限制性。而在聯邦層級，大規
模的新監管措施即使被國會提出，也很可能被川普否決，且AI競爭力仍是跨黨派國策，建設趨勢將持續推進。

©  CTBC
AI 資料中心已從「產業政策」轉變為「民生電價議題」
超大型業者算力供給轉吃緊
產業影響
2030年電力缺口收斂
6
• 德州資料中心暫停令，AI算力布局的轉變與供需影響：德州8月初頒布的暫停令，同步凍結了新資料中心的興建
（僅限未動工新案）與電力輸送。在扣除被淘汰的虛報專案需求後，預估部分專案將外溢至其他電力更吃緊的州，
導致全美電力缺口淨擴大約 3–6 GW。若Hinojosa 就任後延續暫停令執行超過一年，其影響2028 年全美電力缺
口將進一步擴大，2030年供需缺口預估將逐步收斂。另一方面，暫停令讓2028年本就吃緊的算力市場更緊，維
持算力租金的高檔區間，對超大型業者而言建置成本增加，與手上既有的算力可以提高售價，兩者相抵，2028
年企業獲利的預估影響僅在1-3%水準。
• 提高監管是正在發生的實質逆風，喊停是低機率的極端政治口號：AI 技術攸關美國的核心基本國力。民主黨的
政策向來主張「調整與監管」，而非全面「廢止」。因此，未來更嚴格的許可審查與補助限制，勢必會提高雲

端大廠在德州擴張的時間成本與資本風險。然而，也可能推動針對資料中心的特定稅種，例如聯邦總收入稅，
或針對資料中心電力消耗的消費稅。民主黨也有可能在新會期提出比共和黨更強制性更嚴格監管的版本。民主
黨掌控國會後，最可能成法的是「電費成本分攤」立法，因為川普已將此議題列為自己的政績。

• ACA補貼及醫療補助是最可能與川普達成交易的領域：民主黨贏得參眾兩院，不等於民主黨執政，兩黨仍須協
商。就健護產業而言，相對有利影響的領域在2025年底已到期的<平價醫療法案(ACA)>增強型保費補貼，受淨保
費上升，已有超過200萬人放棄投保，及受<大而美法案>被削減的醫療補助(Medicaid)在2025~2034年超過
9,000億美元。現ACA進行增強型補貼的恢復提案還在進行中，需總統簽署才能立法恢復，可以是談判交換條件
的一部分；Medicaid刪減是已成法、分階段生效，無法被推翻，只能延緩Medicaid的刪減，其中對工作的要求
限制於今年底生效，早於新國會就任，12/11臨時撥款法案談判是可以納入談判作為局部修正空間的時間點。
• 健護是民主黨國會最直接的受惠產業，健護未來一年本益比仍相對合理：預期若醫療補助刪減延後，醫院將受
惠，ACA補貼若部分恢復，將有利ACA型管理式醫療業者的投保人數止跌回升。以健護未來一年本益比來看，

目前股價仍相對合理。

• 期中選舉前股市較為承壓，但無礙趨勢：根據歷史經驗，期中選舉年通常是總統任期中回撤較深的一年，且選
前的股市波動會明顯放大，然在選後不確定性消退，市場表現往往有所改善，標普500指數在選後的3個月，歷
史中位數報酬率為+6%，而分裂政府(總統和國會分屬不同黨派)的歷史報酬率通常較佳。
• AI相關類股波動高，資金輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢：真正驅動市場的因素仍是利率、地緣、
AI，而不是單純從歷史規律推演，近期市場擔憂雲端業者的現金流和債務問題，因此若資料中心的建置時程受
到影響，將進一步影響相關業者的營收和償債時程，提高AI相關類股的波動；另外眾議院是否對川普提出彈劾，

也將造成政治上的波動。但就整體趨勢來說，既有政策不會因此出現重大轉折，穩健成長的企業獲利提供重要
支撐，AI類股修正時期資金仍在其他類股間輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢。

績，上漲比率超越五成。以參眾兩院控制權皆翻轉的1994年、2006年來看，1944年因美聯準會啟動快速升息，
加上墨西哥金融危機爆發，選後新興市場進入近一年空頭走勢。2006年即便也是分裂政府，全球宏觀環境受惠
貿易繁榮、商品超級周期推動經濟成長，新興市場選後12個月累積報酬亮眼。總體來說，選後不確定性下降，
新興市場可望迎反彈走勢，而即便美國政治版圖翻轉，選後新興市場走勢取決於景氣、政策推演與地緣干擾。
• 市場短線待消化資料中心建設進程對北亞出口影響：美期中選舉對新興市場影響較大面向，主要是關稅與貿易
政策、債務上限，以及對資料中心建設的態度。關稅主導權在白宮推估影響有限 ; 預算與債限談判或對利率造
成波動 ; 短線需消化選後對資料中心監管趨嚴影響北亞出口的失望情緒，然預計長線AI建置仍屬國家重心政策。

• 期中選舉共和黨大敗，美元走弱：週三市場確認共和黨於期中選舉失利後，美元兌主要貨幣全面回檔。本次期
中選舉由民主黨取得參、眾兩院多數。民主黨掌控參議院後，可阻止或延後川普的人事任命；贏得眾議院後，
則可對政府展開調查，甚至進一步推動彈劾程序。歷史經驗顯示，當民主黨於期中選舉後同時掌控參、眾兩院
時，美元指數平均下跌4.6%，6次中有4次下跌。整體而言，選舉結果短期內可能因預算協商及債務上限僵局

升高政治風險。2027年美國將再度面臨債務上限問題，美伊地緣政治及貿易戰等變數亦將牽動實質利率走勢。
• 2027年債務上限恐成兩黨鬥爭焦點，不利美元：12月11日前，現任國會通過持續撥款決議的可能性偏高，但
美國聯邦政府收支預計將於2027年觸及債務上限。受債務上限約束的債務規模預估於2027年3月接近法定上限
500億美元以內，之後隨4月稅收入帳而暫時下降；但若財政赤字增幅高於預期，X-Day恐提前到來。展望
2027年，美國財政議題仍將面臨逆風。共和黨失去完全執政雖有助抑制財政擴張，但政治僵局恐加劇債務上限、
違約及信評遭調降風險，使美元波動加大。

## Document 510: メキシコ中銀は４会合ぶりの利下げ実施も、「一度限りの調整」を強調 ～インフレ懸念が残るなかで長期の金利据え置きも、ペソ相_0380ea99

Source PDF: all report pdf/メキシコ中銀は４会合ぶりの利下げ実施も、「一度限りの調整」を強調 ～インフレ懸念が残るなかで長期の金利据え置きも、ペソ相_0380ea99.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

World Trends

2026 年 11 月 6 日

1/5 
メキシコ中銀は４会合ぶりの利下げ実施も、「一度限りの調整」を強調 
～インフレ懸念が残るなかで長期の金利据え置きも、ペソ相場は上値の重い展開が続くか～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ メキシコ銀行（中銀）は11 月５日の定例会合で、政策金利を 25bp 引き下げて 6.25％とする
ことを決定した。４会合ぶりの利下げは市場予想に反するものであったものの、中銀は今回
の決定を「一度限りの調整」とし、先行きは長期間にわたって金利を据え置く方針を示した。
ＦＲＢは金融引き締め姿勢を維持している一方、メキシコ中銀は景気・物価動向を重視した
独自の政策判断を継続している。 
○ 足元のメキシコ経済は、ＡＩ・半導体関連需要を背景とする輸出拡大などを追い風に持ち直
している。しかし、先行きには米国の通商政策を巡る不透明感などを理由に、下振れリスク
が残る。一方で、足元のインフレ率には再び加速する兆しが出ており、基調的な物価上昇圧
力も根強いことから、先行きの追加利下げのハードルは高いと考えられる。 
○ 先行きの金融政策は、物価や景気動向に加え、ＦＲＢの政策運営やペソ相場を見極める様子
見姿勢が続くと見込まれる。ペソ相場については、国内景気の持ち直しや中銀の慎重姿勢が
下支えとなる一方、米国との金利差縮小や USMCA の再交渉を巡る不透明感などが重しとなる
展開が見込まれる。このため、当面のペソ相場は上値の重い展開が続くと予想される。

### Page 2

2/5 
【メキシコ中銀は４会合ぶりに「一度限りの」利下げ決定、政策金利は４年半ぶりの低水準に】 
メキシコ銀行（中央銀行）は、11 月５日に開催した定例の金融政策決定会合において、政策金利
である翌日物銀行間金利の誘導目標を
25bp 引き下げ、6.25％とすることを決定
した（図１）。同行は、2025 年から金融
緩和を進めてきたものの、今年５月の定
例会合での利下げを最後に、６月、８月、
９月は３会合連続で政策金利を 6.50％
に据え置いた。このため、今回の決定は
４会合ぶりの利下げとなる。今回の決定
は、５人の政策委員のうちロドリゲス総
裁ら３人の賛成による多数決で採択さ
れたものの、残る２人は金利の据え置き
を主張するなど、金融政策を巡る意見の相違も明らかとなった。市場の事前予想では据え置き予想
が大勢を占めていたため、今回の決定は予想外の利下げとなった。 
会合後に公表された声明文では、今回の利下げについて「一度限りの調整（ajuste puntual）」と
位置付けるとともに、先行きについて「政策金利を現行水準で長期間にわたり据え置くことが、イ
ンフレの目標への収束、対外的なマクロ経済が招く課題への対応の観点でも適切」との考えを明示
した。このことは、今回の決定が継続的な金融緩和局面への回帰を意味するものではなく、国内の
景気や物価動向を踏まえて金融引き締めの程度を微調整する性格の強いものであったことを示唆
していると考えられる。

【FRBは金融引き締め姿勢を維持する一方、メキシコ中銀は「独自の政策判断」を継続する意向】 
今回の利下げ決定を巡っては、米国とメキシコの金融政策の方向性が異なっていることが注目さ
れる。ＦＲＢ（米連邦準備制度理事会）は、９月のFOMC（連邦公開市場委員会）において、政策金
利（ＦＦ金利）を 25bp 引き上げて 3.75～4.00％とすることを決定した。10 月会合では据え置いた
ものの、米国経済の底堅さや物価上昇圧力の根強さを背景に、金融引き締め姿勢を維持している。
一方、メキシコ中銀は９月の定例会合で政策金利を 6.50％に据え置くとともに、先行きの政策運営
については、両国のマクロ経済環境の違いを理由に、ＦＲＢによる政策金利の調整に機械的に反応
する必要はないとの考えを示していた（注１）。今回発表された声明文では、９月会合の声明文で示
された前述の文言は繰り返されなかった。しかし、利下げに動いたことで、両国の経済環境の違い
を踏まえた独自の政策判断を実行に移したと捉えられる。 
こうした判断の背景には、米国では堅調な内需が物価上昇圧力を招いている一方、メキシコでは

注１ ９月25 日付レポート「メキシコ中銀、政策判断の自由度を重視」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666044.html） 
図 1 政策金利の推移

（出所）CEIC より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成
